	TUR_declare_jihad = {
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_nationalism
		allowed = {
			original_tag = TUR
		}
		available = {
			is_great_war_ongoing = yes
			any_enemy_country = {
				is_major = yes
			}	
			has_civil_war = no
		}
		visible = {
			great_power_politics_great_war_visible = yes
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = TUR_abolished_caliph
			}
		}
		cost = 25
		fire_only_once = yes
		complete_effect = {
			add_war_support = 0.05
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					controller = {
						OR = {
							original_tag = ROOT
							is_ally_with = TUR
						}
					}
					OR = {
						is_arabian_core = yes
						is_core_of = PER
						is_core_of = ALB
						is_core_of = EGY
					}	
					has_resistance = yes
				}
				add_compliance = 5
			}
			every_enemy_country = {
				every_controlled_state = {
					limit = {
						has_resistance = yes
						OR = {
							is_arabian_core = yes
							is_core_of = PER
							is_core_of = ALB
							is_core_of = EGY
						}
					}
					add_resistance = 15
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = TUR_jihad_declared
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 1000
		}
	}

	TUR_invoke_senussiya_alliance = {
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_nationalism
		allowed = {
			original_tag = TUR
		}
		fire_only_once = yes
		visible = {
			great_power_politics_great_war_visible = yes
			is_subject = no
			is_great_war_ongoing = yes
			FROM = {
				is_subject = no
				is_in_faction = no
				OR = {
					has_government = neutrality
					has_government = fascism
				}	
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = TUR
					has_war_together_with = TUR
					has_country_flag = LBA_modus_vivendi_of_acroma
				}
			}
		}
		targets = {
			LBA
		}
		available = {
			has_war_with = ITA
			has_war_with = ENG	
			ENG = {
				OR = {
					is_neighbor_of = FROM
					any_subject_country = {
						is_neighbor_of = FROM
					}
				}
			}
		}
		days_remove = 14
		remove_effect = {	
			LBA = {
				country_event = ww1_libya.7
				set_country_flag = TUR_invoke_senussiya_alliance
			}	
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 15
			modifier = {
				has_war_with = ITA
				factor = 1000
			}
			modifier = {
				date < 1915.11.1
				factor = 0
			}
		}
	}

TUR_minority_decision = {
	TUR_settle_turks_in_thrace_decision = {
		icon = GFX_decision_eng_blackshirt_march
		allowed = {
			original_tag = TUR
		}
		cost = 30
		state_target = yes
		target_array = ROOT.controlled_states
		target_trigger = {
			FROM = {
				is_ottoman_thrace_state = yes
			}
		}
		on_map_mode = map_only
		days_remove = 35
		available = {
		}
		visible = {
			has_completed_focus = TUR_settle_turks_in_thrace
			FROM = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						has_state_flag = TUR_greek_state
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_greek_minority }
					}
					AND = {
						has_state_flag = TUR_southslav_state
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_southslav_minority }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		complete_effect = {
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				limit = {
					region = 129
					is_core_of = ROOT
				}
				prioritize = {
					854
				}
				add_manpower = -15000
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			FROM = {
				add_manpower = 15000
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_state_flag = TUR_greek_state
					}
					remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_greek_minority }
					clr_state_flag = TUR_greek_state
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_state_flag = TUR_southslav_state
					}
					remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_southslav_minority }
					clr_state_flag = TUR_southslav_state
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				ottoman_minority_variable_check = yes
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				add = 10
				FROM = {
					any_neighbor_state = {
						has_state_flag = TUR_ottoman_minority_state

						is_in_faction_with = FROM
					}
					any_owned_state = {
						is_in_home_area = yes
					}
					is_neighbor_of = TUR
				}
			}
		}
		cost = 20
		fire_only_once = no
		days_remove = 15
		visible = {
			FROM = {
				exists = yes
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = great_war_loser
				}
				OR = {
					tag = GER
					tag = ENG
					AND = {
						tag = FRA
						TUR = { has_completed_focus = TUR_new_weapon_contracts }
					}
				}
			}
			has_variable = opda_consumer_goods
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_focus_tree = kostantiniyye_focus_tree
				}
				has_completed_focus = TUR_purchase_guns
			}
			NOT = {
				has_focus_tree = turkey_focus_tree
				has_completed_focus = TUR_domestic_weapon_companies
			}
		}
		complete_effect = {
			set_country_flag = {
				flag = has_active_purchase_contract
				days = 15
				value = 1
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					FROM = {
						tag = GER
					}
					ROOT = { has_completed_focus = TUR_german_ottoman_arms_deal }
				}
				TUR_add_debt_0098 = yes
			}
			else = {
				TUR_add_debt_0148 = yes
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			clr_country_flag = has_active_purchase_contract
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_completed_focus = TUR_import_dependency
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = infantry_equipment
					amount = 5000
					producer = FROM
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = infantry_equipment
					amount = 3000
					producer = FROM
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				OR = {
					num_of_civilian_factories < 4
					stockpile_ratio = {
						archetype = infantry_equipment
						ratio > 1
					}
					
				}
				NOT = {
					has_global_flag = great_war
				}
				has_war = no
				factor = 0
			}
			modifier = {
				has_global_flag = TUR_must_won_bw1
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = BW1_winner
					date > 1914.1.1
				}
				factor = 30
			}
			modifier = {
				has_global_flag = TUR_must_won_bw1
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = BW1_winner
					date > 1914.1.1
				}
				stockpile_ratio = {
					archetype = infantry_equipment
					ratio < 1 
				}
				factor = 270
			}
			modifier = {
				has_war = no
				has_equipment = {
					infantry_equipment < 1500
				}
				is_great_war_ongoing = yes
				factor = 150
			}
			modifier = {
				has_war = yes
				has_equipment = {
					infantry_equipment < 1500
				}
				factor = 1500

TUR_reclaiming_ahd_i_milli = {
	TUR_border_skirmishes = {
		icon = border_war

		target_array = neighbors

		available = {
			FROM = {
				any_owned_state = {
					is_controlled_by = FROM
					any_neighbor_state = {
						is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
						set_temp_variable = { var:attacker_state_temp = id }
						has_ROOT_at_least_1_div_in_current_state_scope = yes
						hidden_trigger = { NOT = { has_state_flag = border_incident_active } }
					}
					NOT = { has_state_flag = border_incident_active }
					set_temp_variable = { var:defender_state_temp = id }
				}
			}
			NOT = { has_non_aggression_pact_with = FROM }
		}

		visible = {
			has_completed_focus = TUR_disobey_the_treaties
			FROM = { exists = yes }
			NOT = { has_war_with = FROM }
			FROM = { has_not_initiated_border_incident_with_ROOT = yes }
			FROM = {
				NOT = {
					is_subject_of = ROOT
				}
				NOT = {
					is_in_faction_with = ROOT
				}
			}
			NOT = {
				is_subject_of = FROM
			}

			NOT = { has_border_war_with = FROM }
			FROM = {
				any_owned_state = {
					OR = {
						is_claimed_by = ROOT
						is_core_of = ROOT
					}
					hidden_trigger = { NOT = { has_state_flag = border_incident_active } }
					any_neighbor_state = {
						hidden_trigger = { NOT = { has_state_flag = border_incident_active } }
						is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 0
		}

		cost = 50

		fire_only_once = no

		days_remove = 7
		days_re_enable = 15

		complete_effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				FROM = {
					random_owned_controlled_state = {
						limit = {
							is_claimed_by = ROOT
							hidden_trigger = { NOT = { has_state_flag = border_incident_active } }
							any_neighbor_state = {
								hidden_trigger = { NOT = { has_state_flag = border_incident_active } }
								has_ROOT_at_least_1_div_in_current_state_scope = yes
								is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
							}
						}
						save_event_target_as = defender_state
						set_variable = { FROM.defender_state_vs_@ROOT = id }
						set_temp_variable = { defender_state_temp = id }
						set_state_flag = border_incident_active
					}
				}
				random_state = {
					limit = {
						is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
						hidden_trigger = { NOT = { has_state_flag = border_incident_active } }
						has_ROOT_at_least_1_div_in_current_state_scope = yes
						any_neighbor_state = {
							state = var:FROM.defender_state_vs_@ROOT
						}
					}
					save_event_target_as = attacker_state
					set_variable = { FROM.attacker_state_vs_@ROOT = id }
					set_temp_variable = { attacker_state_temp = id }
					set_state_flag = border_incident_active
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			start_border_war = {
				change_state_after_war = yes
				attacker = {
					state = var:FROM.attacker_state_vs_@ROOT
					num_provinces = 4
					on_win = wtt_border_conflict_won_attacker.1
					on_lose = wtt_border_conflict_lost_attacker.1
					on_cancel = wtt_border_conflict_canceled_attacker.1
				}
				
				defender = {
					state = var:FROM.defender_state_vs_@ROOT
					num_provinces = 4
					on_win = wtt_border_conflict_won_defender.1
					on_lose = wtt_border_conflict_lost_defender.1
					on_cancel = wtt_border_conflict_canceled_defender.1
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_border_conflict_defender_vs_FROM = yes }
				var:attacker_state_vs_@FROM = {
					clr_state_flag = border_incident_active
				}
				var:defender_state_vs_@FROM = {
					clr_state_flag = border_incident_active
				}
				clear_variable = attacker_state_vs_@FROM
				clear_variable = defender_state_vs_@FROM
			}
			else = {
				FROM = {
					var:attacker_state_vs_@ROOT = {
						clr_state_flag = border_incident_active
					}
					var:defender_state_vs_@ROOT = {
						clr_state_flag = border_incident_active
					}
					clear_variable = attacker_state_vs_@ROOT
					clear_variable = defender_state_vs_@ROOT
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			any_country = {
				OR = {
					tag = ROOT
					is_ally_with = ROOT
				}
				owns_state = 939
				controls_state = 939
				owns_state = 823
				controls_state = 823
			}
		}
		available = {
			num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects > 1
		}
		fire_only_once = no
		days_remove = 30
		modifier = {
			civilian_factory_use = 1
		}
		complete_effect = {
			build_railway = {
				path = { 6997 4660 9715 1610 1962 }
				level = 1
			}
		}
	}
}

							}
						}
					}
				}
			}

		}

			base = 0
			modifier = {
				date > 1914.1.10
				add = 1000
			}
		}
	}
